&-\frac{1}{9}\varepsilon{}_{i j}w{}^{a b}\left.W{}_{a b \alpha k}\right|\varepsilon{}^{j i}\chi{}^{\alpha k} +\frac{1}{12}w{}^{a b}\left.W{}_{a b \alpha i}\right|\chi{}^{\alpha i}\\
&-\frac{1}{12}w{}^{a b}\left.W{}_{a b \alpha i}\right|\chi{}^{\alpha i} +\frac{i}{96}\varepsilon{}_{i j}w{}^{a b}\left.W{}_{a b}{}^{\alpha}{}_{k}\right|\varepsilon{}^{j i}(\Gamma{}^{c}){}_{\beta \alpha}\left.\mathcal R(\mathbb D){}_{c}{}^{\beta k}\right|\\
&+\frac{i}{48}\varepsilon{}_{i j}(\Sigma{}^{a b}){}^{\alpha \beta}\left.W{}_{\gamma \alpha \beta k}\right|\left.W{}_{a b}{}^{\delta k}\right|\varepsilon{}^{i j}w{}^{\gamma}{}_{\delta} +\frac{1}{9}\varepsilon{}_{i j}(\Sigma{}^{a b}){}_{\alpha}{}^{\beta}\chi{}_{\beta k}\left.W{}_{a b}{}^{\gamma k}\right|\varepsilon{}^{i j}w{}^{\alpha}{}_{\gamma}\\
&+\frac{1}{9}\varepsilon{}_{i j}(\Sigma{}^{a b}){}^{\alpha}{}_{\beta}\chi{}_{\alpha k}\left.W{}_{a b}{}^{\gamma k}\right|\varepsilon{}^{i j}w{}^{\beta}{}_{\gamma} -\frac{i}{96}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\varepsilon{}_{k l}w{}^{a b}(\Sigma{}_{a b}){}^{\alpha \beta}\varepsilon{}^{l k}\left.W{}_{\gamma \alpha \beta}{}^{\delta}\right|\varepsilon{}^{i j}w{}^{\gamma}{}_{\delta}\\
&+\frac{1}{96}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\varepsilon{}_{k l}w{}^{a b}(\Sigma{}_{a b}){}^{\alpha \beta}\varepsilon{}^{l k}(\Gamma{}^{c}){}_{\gamma \alpha}\big(\nabla_{c}w{}_{\beta}{}^{\delta}\big)\varepsilon{}^{i j}w{}^{\gamma}{}_{\delta} +\frac{1}{72}\varepsilon{}_{i j}w{}^{a b}(\Sigma{}_{a b}){}_{\alpha}{}^{\beta}\Phi{}_{\beta}{}^{\gamma}{}_{k}{}^{k}\varepsilon{}^{i j}w{}^{\alpha}{}_{\gamma}\\
&+\frac{1}{96}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\varepsilon{}_{k l}w{}^{a b}(\Sigma{}_{a b}){}^{\alpha \beta}\varepsilon{}^{l k}(\Gamma{}^{c}){}_{\gamma \beta}\big(\nabla_{c}w{}_{\alpha}{}^{\delta}\big)\varepsilon{}^{i j}w{}^{\gamma}{}_{\delta} +\frac{1}{72}\varepsilon{}_{i j}w{}^{a b}(\Sigma{}_{a b}){}^{\alpha}{}_{\beta}\Phi{}_{\alpha}{}^{\gamma}{}_{k}{}^{k}\varepsilon{}^{i j}w{}^{\beta}{}_{\gamma}\\
&+\frac{1}{96}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\varepsilon{}_{k l}w{}^{a b}(\Sigma{}_{a b}){}^{\alpha \beta}\varepsilon{}^{l k}(\Gamma{}^{c}){}_{\gamma}{}^{\delta}\big(\nabla_{c}w{}_{\alpha \beta}\big)\varepsilon{}^{i j}w{}^{\gamma}{}_{\delta} -\frac{1}{72}\varepsilon{}_{i j}w{}^{a b}(\Sigma{}_{a b}){}^{\alpha \beta}\Phi{}_{\alpha \beta k}{}^{k}\varepsilon{}^{i j}w{}^{\gamma}{}_{\gamma}\\
&+\frac{1}{192}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\varepsilon{}_{k l}w{}^{a b}(\Sigma{}_{a b}){}_{\alpha}{}^{\beta}\varepsilon{}^{l k}(\Gamma{}^{c}){}_{\beta}{}^{\gamma}\big(\nabla_{c}w{}^{\delta}{}_{\gamma}\big)\varepsilon{}^{i j}w{}^{\alpha}{}_{\delta} +\frac{1}{192}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\varepsilon{}_{k l}w{}^{a b}(\Sigma{}_{a b}){}_{\alpha}{}^{\beta}\varepsilon{}^{l k}(\Gamma{}^{c}){}^{\gamma \delta}\big(\nabla_{c}w{}_{\beta \delta}\big)\varepsilon{}^{i j}w{}^{\alpha}{}_{\gamma}\\
&+\frac{1}{192}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\varepsilon{}_{k l}w{}^{a b}(\Sigma{}_{a b}){}^{\alpha}{}_{\beta}\varepsilon{}^{l k}(\Gamma{}^{c}){}_{\alpha}{}^{\gamma}\big(\nabla_{c}w{}^{\delta}{}_{\gamma}\big)\varepsilon{}^{i j}w{}^{\beta}{}_{\delta} +\frac{1}{192}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\varepsilon{}_{k l}w{}^{a b}(\Sigma{}_{a b}){}^{\alpha}{}_{\beta}\varepsilon{}^{l k}(\Gamma{}^{c}){}^{\gamma \delta}\big(\nabla_{c}w{}_{\alpha \delta}\big)\varepsilon{}^{i j}w{}^{\beta}{}_{\gamma}\\
&-\frac{1}{192}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\varepsilon{}_{k l}w{}^{a b}(\Sigma{}_{a b}){}^{\alpha \beta}\varepsilon{}^{l k}(\Gamma{}^{c}){}_{\alpha}{}^{\gamma}\big(\nabla_{c}w{}_{\beta \gamma}\big)\varepsilon{}^{i j}w{}^{\delta}{}_{\delta} -\frac{1}{192}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\varepsilon{}_{k l}w{}^{a b}(\Sigma{}_{a b}){}^{\alpha \beta}\varepsilon{}^{l k}(\Gamma{}^{c}){}_{\beta}{}^{\gamma}\big(\nabla_{c}w{}_{\alpha \gamma}\big)\varepsilon{}^{i j}w{}^{\delta}{}_{\delta}
